<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="id">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Buku Induk Siswa – SD NEGERI KARANG TENGAH 12</title>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Newsreader:wght@500;700&family=Public+Sans:wght@400;500;600&display=swap">
<style>
:root{--bg:#eef2f6;--surface:#fff;--ink:#1b2a41;--muted:#5d6b7e;--line:#d5dce6;--accent:#0f766e;--accent-ink:#fff;--danger:#b42318;--chip:#e1ede9;
box-sizing:border-box;padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"]){--bg:#101722;--surface:#18222f;--ink:#e6ecf4;--muted:#93a1b4;--line:#2b394b;--accent:#35b5a8;--accent-ink:#06201d;--danger:#f08a80;--chip:#1d3a37}}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--bg:#101722;--surface:#18222f;--ink:#e6ecf4;--muted:#93a1b4;--line:#2b394b;--accent:#35b5a8;--accent-ink:#06201d;--danger:#f08a80;--chip:#1d3a37}
html{scroll-padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px)}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:15px/1.5 "Public Sans",system-ui,sans-serif}
.wrap{max-width:1100px;margin:0 auto;padding:20px 16px 48px}
h1{font:700 29px/1.15 "Newsreader",Georgia,serif;margin:0}
.sub{color:var(--muted);margin:2px 0 14px}
.top{display:flex;gap:12px;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-end;flex-wrap:wrap}
.stats{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:12px 0}
.stat{background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:6px 14px;min-width:88px}
.stat b{display:block;font:700 22px "Newsreader",serif}
.stat span{color:var(--muted);font-size:13px}
.bar{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px}
input,select,textarea,button{font:inherit;color:var(--ink)}
input,select,textarea{background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px;padding:8px 10px;width:100%}
.bar input{flex:1 1 220px;width:auto}.bar select{width:auto}
button{border:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--surface);border-radius:6px;padding:8px 14px;cursor:pointer}
button.pri{background:var(--accent);color:var(--accent-ink);border-color:var(--accent);font-weight:600}
button.del{color:var(--danger)}
:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--accent);outline-offset:2px}
.tbl{overflow-x:auto;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px}
table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;min-width:700px}
th,td{text-align:left;padding:9px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
th{font-weight:600;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;white-space:nowrap}
tbody tr{cursor:pointer}tbody tr:hover{background:var(--bg)}
.chip{display:inline-block;background:var(--chip);border-radius:99px;padding:1px 10px;font-size:13px}
.empty{padding:40px 16px;text-align:center;color:var(--muted)}
.pg{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:10px;color:var(--muted);flex-wrap:wrap}
dialog{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--surface);color:var(--ink);padding:0;width:min(720px,94vw);max-height:92vh}
dialog::backdrop{background:rgba(10,16,26,.55)}
.dh{padding:14px 20px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px}
.dh h2{font:700 22px "Newsreader",serif;margin:0}
.db{padding:14px 20px;overflow:auto;max-height:68vh}
.df{padding:12px 20px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end;flex-wrap:wrap}
.grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:12px}
label{display:block;font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:3px}
fieldset{border:0;border-top:1px solid var(--line);margin:14px 0 0;padding:10px 0 0}
legend{font-weight:600;padding-right:8px}
.kv{display:grid;grid-template-columns:170px 1fr;gap:5px 12px;margin:0 0 6px}
.kv dt{color:var(--muted)}.kv dd{margin:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.sec{font:700 17px "Newsreader",serif;margin:14px 0 6px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding-bottom:4px}
@media(max-width:560px){.grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.kv{grid-template-columns:1fr}.kv dt{margin-top:6px}}
.nav{display:flex;gap:4px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);margin:4px 0 12px}
.nav button{border:0;border-bottom:3px solid transparent;border-radius:0;background:none;padding:8px 14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--muted)}
.nav button[aria-pressed="true"]{color:var(--ink);border-bottom-color:var(--accent)}
#vRekap table{min-width:420px}#vRekap td.n,#vRekap th.n{text-align:right}
#vRekap tr.sub td{background:var(--chip);font-weight:600}
#vRekap tr.tot td{font-weight:700;border-top:2px solid var(--ink)}
#vRekap tr[data-r]:hover{background:var(--bg)}
:root{--band:#12364a}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"]){--band:#0c2330}}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--band:#0c2330}
body{background:var(--bg)}
.top{background:var(--band);color:#fff;padding:22px 24px;border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:16px;align-items:center}
.top h1{color:#fff;font-size:32px}.top .sub{color:#b9cfdc;margin:6px 0 0}
.top button{background:rgba(255,255,255,.12);border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.3);color:#fff}
.top button.pri{background:#fff;color:#12364a;border-color:#fff}
.nav{border:0;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 14px}
.nav button{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:99px;background:var(--surface);padding:7px 16px}
.nav button[aria-pressed="true"]{background:var(--accent);color:var(--accent-ink);border-color:var(--accent)}
.stat{border-left:4px solid var(--accent);box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(18,54,74,.1)}
.tbl{border-radius:12px;box-shadow:0 1px 3px rgba(18,54,74,.12)}
thead th{background:var(--chip);color:var(--ink)}
tbody tr:nth-child(even){background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--bg) 55%,transparent)}
.cards{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(290px,1fr));gap:14px}
.card{background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:14px 16px;box-shadow:0 1px 3px rgba(18,54,74,.1)}
.card h3{font:700 17px "Newsreader",serif;margin:0 0 8px}
.hb{display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(80px,150px) 1fr 40px;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin:5px 0;font-size:14px}
.hb i{display:block;height:12px;border-radius:6px;background:var(--accent);min-width:4px}.hb b{text-align:right}
#toast{position:fixed;left:50%;bottom:calc(20px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px));transform:translateX(-50%);background:var(--band);color:#fff;padding:10px 18px;border-radius:8px;z-index:50;display:none;max-width:90vw;box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.3)}
body.locked .wrap{display:none}
#login{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:100;background:var(--band);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:20px;overflow:auto}
#login[hidden]{display:none}
.lbox{background:var(--surface);color:var(--ink);border-radius:16px;padding:30px 28px;width:min(420px,100%);box-shadow:0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.35);margin:auto}
.lbox h1{font-size:26px}.lbox label{margin-top:12px}.lbox .sub{margin-bottom:6px}
#lMsg{color:var(--danger);min-height:1.5em;margin:10px 0 8px;font-size:14px}
.su[hidden]{display:none}
:root{--bg:#ebedf7;--soft:#e4eefc}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"]){--soft:#1c2a40}}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--soft:#1c2a40}
.layout{display:flex;min-height:100vh}
.side{width:236px;flex:none;background:#1b1c2e;color:#aab0c6;position:sticky;top:0;height:100vh;overflow:auto;padding:18px 0}
.brand{color:#fff;font:700 17px "Public Sans",sans-serif;letter-spacing:.2em;padding:6px 22px}
.bsub{color:#7f86a3;font-size:12px;padding:0 22px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid #2a2c42}
.grp{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.08em;color:#6c7391;padding:18px 22px 6px}
.side nav button{display:flex;width:100%;gap:12px;align-items:center;border:0;border-radius:0;background:none;color:#c3c8dc;padding:10px 22px;text-align:left}
.side nav button::before{content:"";width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:3px;background:#5b6180;flex:none}
.side nav button:hover{background:#242640}
.side nav button[aria-pressed="true"]{background:#242640;color:#fff;box-shadow:inset 3px 0 0 #7c4dff}
.side nav button[aria-pressed="true"]::before{background:#7c4dff}
.main{flex:1;min-width:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column}
.topbar{background:var(--surface);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding:10px 24px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center}
.topbar .lo{border-color:var(--danger);color:var(--danger)}
.hi{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;color:var(--muted)}
.av{width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:6px;background:#c9f1f0;color:#0f766e;display:grid;place-items:center;font-weight:700}
.ptitle{background:var(--surface);padding:14px 24px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.03em;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.main .wrap{flex:1;width:100%;max-width:none;padding:20px 24px}
.foot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:16px 24px;background:var(--surface);border-top:1px solid var(--line);color:var(--muted);font-size:13px}
body.locked .layout{display:none}
.nav{display:none}
.top{background:transparent;color:var(--ink);padding:0;border-radius:0;margin-bottom:14px}
.top>div:first-child,#setBtn,#outBtn{display:none}
.top button{background:var(--surface);border-color:var(--line);color:var(--ink)}
.top button.pri{background:var(--accent);color:var(--accent-ink);border-color:var(--accent)}
body[data-tab]:not([data-tab="vData"]) .top{display:none}
.dgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.2fr 1fr;gap:20px}
.dcard{background:var(--soft);border-radius:14px;padding:18px 20px}
.dcard h3{font:600 15px "Public Sans",sans-serif;margin:0 0 8px}
.legend{display:flex;gap:16px;justify-content:center;flex-wrap:wrap;color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;margin-top:6px}
.legend i{display:inline-block;width:11px;height:11px;border-radius:3px;margin-right:6px}
.two{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:16px;margin-bottom:16px}
.big{border-radius:12px;color:#fff;padding:18px;min-height:150px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between}
.big svg{opacity:.9}.big b{font:700 30px "Newsreader",serif;display:block}.big span{font-weight:600;font-size:14px}
.b1{background:#8b4dff}.b2{background:#f64f6c}
.four{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:16px}
.sm{background:var(--soft);border-radius:12px;padding:16px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px}
.sm b{font:700 28px "Newsreader",serif}.sm span{font-weight:600;font-size:14px}
@media(max-width:900px){.dgrid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.layout{flex-direction:column}.side{width:100%;height:auto;position:static;padding:10px 0}.side nav{display:flex;overflow-x:auto}.side nav button{width:auto;white-space:nowrap;padding:8px 14px}.grp,.bsub{display:none}.brand{padding:0 16px 8px}}
.av{cursor:pointer;overflow:hidden}.av img,.bigav img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block}.bigav{overflow:hidden}.fbtn{display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:center;margin:0 0 10px}.fbtn button{font-size:13px;padding:6px 12px}
.pgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:320px 1fr;gap:20px;align-items:start}
.pc{text-align:center}.bigav{width:96px;height:96px;border-radius:50%;background:#c9f1f0;color:#0f766e;font:700 40px "Newsreader",serif;display:grid;place-items:center;margin:6px auto 10px}
.pst{display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:28px;margin:12px 0}.pst b{display:block;font:700 24px "Newsreader",serif}.pst span{color:var(--muted);font-size:13px}
.pc .kv{text-align:left;grid-template-columns:110px 1fr;margin-top:14px}
@media(max-width:900px){.pgrid{grid-template-columns:1fr}}

#printArea{display:none}
.pg-bi{width:210mm;height:296mm;overflow:hidden;padding:11mm 16mm;box-sizing:border-box;background:#fff;color:#000;font:11pt/1.35 "Public Sans",Arial,sans-serif;page-break-after:always;break-after:page}
.pg-bi:last-child{page-break-after:auto;break-after:auto}
.pg-bi .kop{text-align:center;border-bottom:3px double #000;padding-bottom:6px;margin-bottom:8px}
.pg-bi .kop b{display:block;font:700 15pt "Newsreader",Georgia,serif;letter-spacing:.03em}
.pg-bi .kop span{font-size:9.5pt}
.pg-bi h2{font:700 13pt "Newsreader",Georgia,serif;text-align:center;margin:8px 0 2px;letter-spacing:.08em}
.pg-bi .no{text-align:center;font-size:10pt;margin-bottom:8px}
.pg-bi .bitop{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start}
.pg-bi .foto{flex:none;width:30mm;height:40mm;border:1px solid #000;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:8.5pt;text-align:center;color:#444}
.pg-bi .bitop .tb{flex:1}
.pg-bi h4{margin:6px 0 2px;font:700 10.5pt "Public Sans",Arial,sans-serif;border-bottom:1px solid #000;padding-bottom:1px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em}
.pg-bi tr,.kartu tr{background:none!important}
.pg-bi table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;min-width:0}
.pg-bi td{border:0;padding:.5px 4px .5px 0;vertical-align:top;font-size:10pt}
.pg-bi td:first-child{width:46mm;white-space:nowrap}
.pg-bi td:nth-child(2){width:3mm}
.pg-bi .duo{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:2px 8mm}
.pg-bi .duo3{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:2px 4mm}
.pg-bi .duo td:first-child{width:19mm}
.pg-bi .duo3 td{font-size:8.5pt}
.pg-bi .duo3 td:first-child{width:15mm}
.pg-bi .duo b{font-size:9.5pt}
.pg-bi .ttd{margin-top:5mm;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:10pt;page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid}
.pg-bi .ttd div{width:70mm;text-align:center}
.pg-bi .ttd .sp{height:13mm}
.kartuWrap{width:210mm;height:296mm;overflow:hidden;padding:5mm 9mm;box-sizing:border-box;display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,85.6mm);grid-auto-rows:54mm;gap:3mm 8mm;align-content:start;justify-content:center;page-break-after:always;break-after:page;background:#fff}
.kartuWrap:last-child{page-break-after:auto;break-after:auto}
.kartu{width:85.6mm;height:54mm;border:1px solid #000;border-radius:3mm;overflow:hidden;display:flex;flex-direction:column;background:#fff;color:#000;font-family:"Public Sans",Arial,sans-serif;box-sizing:border-box}
.kartu .kh{background:#12364a;color:#fff;padding:1.6mm 3mm;text-align:center;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}
.kartu .kh b{display:block;font-size:8pt;letter-spacing:.06em}
.kartu .kh span{font-size:6pt}
.kartu .kb{display:flex;gap:2.5mm;padding:2mm 3mm;flex:1}
.kartu .kf{flex:none;width:19mm;height:25mm;border:.5mm solid #888;font-size:6pt;color:#666;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center}
.kartu .kd{flex:1;min-width:0}
.kartu .kd .nm{font:700 8.5pt "Public Sans",Arial,sans-serif;line-height:1.15;margin-bottom:1mm;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.kartu table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;min-width:0}
.kartu td{border:0;padding:0 1mm 0 0;font-size:6.6pt;line-height:1.35;vertical-align:top}
.kartu td:first-child{width:11mm;white-space:nowrap}
.kartu .kft{text-align:center;font-size:5.5pt;color:#555;padding:0 0 1.2mm}
@page{size:A4;margin:0}
@media print{
html,body{background:#fff!important}
body.printing .layout,body.printing #login,body.printing #toast,body.printing dialog{display:none!important}
body.printing #printArea{display:block!important}
}

.pg-bi .kop{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4mm}
.pg-bi .kop .kt{flex:1;text-align:center}
.pg-bi .kop .kt span{display:block}
.pg-bi .kop .k1{font-weight:700;font-size:11pt}
.pg-bi .kop img{width:20mm;height:20mm;object-fit:contain;flex:none}
.pg-bi .kop .kx{width:20mm;flex:none}
.lnk{background:none;border:0;padding:4px;color:var(--accent,#0f766e);text-decoration:underline;cursor:pointer;font-size:14px;min-width:0}
</style>
</head>
<body class="locked">
<div class="layout"><aside class="side"><div class="brand">BUKU INDUK</div><div class="bsub" id="sbSchool"></div>
<nav><div class="grp">MENU UTAMA</div>
<button type="button" data-tab="vDash" aria-pressed="true">Dashboard</button>
<button type="button" data-tab="vData" aria-pressed="false">Data Siswa</button>
<button type="button" data-tab="vRekap" aria-pressed="false">Jumlah Siswa</button>
<button type="button" data-tab="vStat" aria-pressed="false">Statistik</button>
<div class="grp">DOKUMEN DAN BUKU INDUK</div>
<button type="button" data-tab="vMut" aria-pressed="false">Mutasi Siswa</button>
<button type="button" id="sbImp">Tambah dari Excel</button>
<button type="button" data-tab="vCad" aria-pressed="false">Cadangan Data</button>
<div class="grp">KEPEGAWAIAN</div>
<button type="button" data-tab="vGtk" aria-pressed="false">Tenaga Pendidik</button>
<button type="button" data-tab="vGtkp" aria-pressed="false">Tenaga Kependidikan</button>
<div class="grp">PENGATURAN</div>
<button type="button" data-tab="vProf" aria-pressed="false">Profil</button>
<button type="button" id="sbSet">Pengaturan Sekolah</button></nav></aside>
<main class="main"><header class="topbar"><button type="button" class="lo" id="tbOut">Logout</button><div class="hi">Hi, <span class="av" id="hiAv">A</span></div></header>
<div class="ptitle" id="pgTitle">DASHBOARD</div>
<div class="wrap">
  <div class="top">
    <div><h1>Buku Induk Siswa</h1><p class="sub"><b id="hSchool" style="color:#fff;font-size:17px"></b> · <span id="hArea"></span><br>Data diimpor dari Dapodik (unduhan 2026-10-07). Perubahan tersimpan di browser ini.</p></div>
    <div><button class="pri" id="add">Tambah siswa</button> <button id="impBtn">Tambah dari Excel</button> <button id="csv">Salin CSV</button><input type="file" id="impFile" accept=".xlsx,.xls" hidden> <button id="reset">Kembalikan data awal</button> <button id="setBtn">Pengaturan</button> <button id="outBtn">Keluar</button></div>
  </div>
  <div class="nav" role="tablist"><button type="button" data-tab="vData" aria-pressed="true">Data siswa</button><button type="button" data-tab="vRekap" aria-pressed="false">Jumlah siswa</button><button type="button" data-tab="vStat" aria-pressed="false">Statistik</button><button type="button" data-tab="vMut" aria-pressed="false">Mutasi</button><button type="button" data-tab="vCad" aria-pressed="false">Cadangan data</button></div>
  <div id="vData"><div class="stats" id="stats"></div>
  <div class="bar">
    <input id="q" type="search" placeholder="Cari nama, NIPD, NISN, atau NIK" aria-label="Cari siswa">
    <select id="fk" aria-label="Filter kelas"></select>
    <button type="button" id="prAll">Cetak...</button><select id="fj" aria-label="Filter jenis kelamin"><option value="">L dan P</option><option value="L">Laki-laki</option><option value="P">Perempuan</option></select>
  </div>
  <div class="tbl"><table><thead><tr><th>No</th><th>Nama</th><th>NIPD</th><th>NISN</th><th>L/P</th><th>Tanggal lahir</th><th>Rombel</th></tr></thead><tbody id="rows"></tbody></table><div class="empty" id="empty" hidden></div></div>
  <div class="pg"><span id="info"></span><span><button id="prev">Sebelumnya</button> <button id="next">Berikutnya</button></span></div>
  </div>
  <div id="vDash" hidden></div>
  <div id="vProf" hidden></div>
  <div id="vGtk" hidden></div>
  <div id="vGtkp" hidden></div>
  <div id="vStat" hidden></div>
  <div id="vMut" hidden></div>
  <div id="vCad" hidden><p class="sub">Data tersimpan di browser ini. Salin teks cadangan di bawah dan simpan di file, atau tempel cadangan sebelumnya untuk memulihkan data.</p><textarea id="bk" rows="8"></textarea><p><button class="pri" id="bkRestore">Pulihkan dari teks ini</button></p></div>
  <div id="vRekap" hidden>
    <p class="sub">Jumlah siswa per rombel dan per kelas. Klik baris rombel untuk melihat daftar siswanya.</p>
    <div class="tbl"><table><thead><tr><th>Rombel</th><th class="n">Laki-laki</th><th class="n">Perempuan</th><th class="n">Jumlah</th></tr></thead><tbody id="rekapRows"></tbody></table></div>
  </div>
</div>

<footer class="foot"><span id="ftSchool"></span><span>© 2026 Buku Induk Siswa</span></footer></main></div>
<dialog id="dForm"><form id="f" onsubmit="return false">
  <div class="dh"><h2 id="ft"></h2><button type="button" data-close>Tutup</button></div>
  <div class="db" id="fb"></div>
  <div class="df"><button type="button" data-close>Batal</button><button class="pri" type="submit">Simpan</button></div>
</form></dialog>

<dialog id="dView">

  <div class="db" id="vb"></div>
  <div class="df"><button class="del" id="vdel">Hapus</button><button id="vcard">Cetak kartu</button><button id="vprint">Cetak buku induk</button><button class="pri" id="vedit">Ubah data</button></div>

<dialog id="dCsv">

</dialog>

<div id="login" hidden><div class="lbox"><h1 id="lSchool"></h1><p class="sub" id="lSub"></p>
<form id="lf"><div class="su"><label for="lSch">Nama sekolah</label><input id="lSch"><label for="lAre">Alamat atau wilayah</label><input id="lAre"></div>
<label for="lUser">Nama pengguna</label><input id="lUser" autocomplete="username"><label for="lPass">Kata sandi</label><input id="lPass" type="password" autocomplete="current-password"><div class="su"><label for="lPass2">Ulangi kata sandi</label><input id="lPass2" type="password" autocomplete="new-password"></div>
<p id="lMsg" role="alert"></p><button class="pri" id="lGo" type="submit" style="width:100%">Masuk</button><p style="text-align:center;margin:12px 0 0"><button type="button" id="lForgot" class="lnk">Lupa kata sandi?</button></p></form></div></div>
<dialog id="dGtk"><form id="gtkF"><div class="dh"><h2 id="gtkT"></h2><button type="button" data-close>Tutup</button></div><div class="db" id="gtkB"></div><div class="df"><button class="del" type="button" id="gtkDel">Hapus</button><button type="button" data-close>Batal</button><button class="pri" type="submit">Simpan</button></div></form></dialog>
<dialog id="dSet"><div class="dh"><h2>Pengaturan sekolah</h2><button type="button" data-close>Tutup</button></div><div class="db"><div class="grid"><div style="grid-column:1/-1"><label for="sSch">Nama sekolah</label><input id="sSch"></div><div style="grid-column:1/-1"><label for="sAre">Alamat atau wilayah</label><input id="sAre"></div><div><label for="sUser">Nama pengguna</label><input id="sUser"></div><div><label for="sNew">Kata sandi baru (kosongkan jika tidak diganti)</label><input id="sNew" type="password" autocomplete="new-password"></div></div></div><div class="df"><button type="button" data-close>Batal</button><button class="pri" type="button" id="sSave">Simpan</button></div></dialog>
<dialog id="dAsk"><div class="dh"><h2>Konfirmasi</h2></div><div class="db" id="askMsg"></div><div class="df"><button type="button" id="askNo">Batal</button><button type="button" class="pri" id="askYes">Ya, lanjutkan</button></div></dialog>
<dialog id="dPrint"><div class="dh"><h2>Cetak</h2><button type="button" data-close>Tutup</button></div><div class="db"><p class="sub" id="prInfo"></p><label for="prType">Jenis cetakan</label><select id="prType"><option value="bi">Buku induk (satu halaman A4 per siswa)</option><option value="kartu">Kartu siswa (10 kartu per lembar A4)</option></select>
<div id="prKop"><h3 class="sec">Kop surat buku induk</h3><div class="grid">
<div style="grid-column:1/-1"><label for="kp1">Baris 1 (instansi, boleh dikosongkan)</label><input id="kp1" placeholder="Contoh: PEMERINTAH KOTA TANGERANG - DINAS PENDIDIKAN"></div>
<div style="grid-column:1/-1"><label for="kp2">Nama sekolah</label><input id="kp2"></div>
<div style="grid-column:1/-1"><label for="kp3">Baris 3 (alamat atau wilayah)</label><input id="kp3"></div>
<div style="grid-column:1/-1"><label for="kp4">Baris 4 (telepon, e-mail, dan sebagainya)</label><input id="kp4"></div>
<div><label for="kpLogo">Logo kiri (boleh dikosongkan)</label><input id="kpLogo" type="file" accept="image/*"><button type="button" id="kpLogoDel" style="margin-top:6px">Hapus logo kiri</button></div>
<div><label for="kpLogoR">Logo kanan (boleh dikosongkan)</label><input id="kpLogoR" type="file" accept="image/*"><button type="button" id="kpLogoRDel" style="margin-top:6px">Hapus logo kanan</button></div>
<div style="grid-column:1/-1"><button type="button" id="kpReset">Kembalikan default</button></div></div>
<label style="margin-top:12px">Pratinjau kop</label><div id="kpPrev"></div></div>

Karang Tengah","15159","Bersama orang tua","Jalan kaki","","","","Tidak","-","RIO MARSHALIN","1984","SMA / sederajat","Karyawan Swasta","Rp. 2,000,000 - Rp. 4,999,999","3671120310840001","FITRI YANTI S.","1985","SMA / sederajat","Tidak bekerja","Tidak Berpenghasilan","3674026306850001","","","PAUD","","","","1 A","","","Tidak","-","0","-","3671-LT-07092020-0083"]]}</script>
<script>
(function(){
var SEED=JSON.parse(document.getElementById("seed").textContent),KEYS=SEED.keys.concat(["status","tglMutasi","sekolahTujuan","alasan","tglMasuk","sekolahAsal","tinggi","berat","kesehatan","prestasi","catatan","bank","noRek","rekNama","layakPip","alasanPip","kebKhusus","anakKe","lintang","bujur","noKK","lingkar","saudara","jarak"]),KEY="buku-induk-karang-tengah-12-v1",PS=50,data=[],page=0,editId=null,viewId=null;
var $=function(i){return document.getElementById(i)};
var S=[
["Identitas",[["nama","Nama lengkap"],["nipd","NIPD"],["nisn","NISN"],["nik","NIK"],["jk","Jenis kelamin","sel",["L","P"]],["agama","Agama"],["tl","Tempat lahir"],["tgl","Tanggal lahir","date"],["akta","No. registrasi akta lahir"]]],
["Alamat dan kontak",[["alamat","Alamat"],["rt","RT"],["rw","RW"],["dusun","Dusun"],["kel","Kelurahan"],["kec","Kecamatan"],["pos","Kode pos"],["tinggal","Jenis tinggal"],["transport","Alat transportasi"],["telp","Telepon"],["hp","HP"],["email","E-mail"]]],
["Data ayah",[["ayNama","Nama"],["ayTh","Tahun lahir"],["ayPend","Pendidikan"],["ayKerja","Pekerjaan"],["ayHasil","Penghasilan"],["ayNik","NIK"]]],
["Data ibu",[["ibNama","Nama"],["ibTh","Tahun lahir"],["ibPend","Pendidikan"],["ibKerja","Pekerjaan"],["ibHasil","Penghasilan"],["ibNik","NIK"]]],
["Data wali",[["wlNama","Nama"],["wlTh","Tahun lahir"],["wlPend","Pendidikan"],["wlKerja","Pekerjaan"],["wlHasil","Penghasilan"],["wlNik","NIK"]]],
["Sekolah dan bantuan",[["rombel","Rombel saat ini"],["noUN","No. peserta ujian"],["ijazah","No. seri ijazah"],["kps","Penerima KPS"],["noKps","No. KPS"],["kip","Penerima KIP"],["noKip","Nomor KIP"],["namaKip","Nama di KIP"],["kks","Nomor KKS"]]],
["Data tambahan Dapodik",[["noKK","No. KK"],["anakKe","Anak ke-"],["saudara","Jumlah saudara kandung"],["jarak","Jarak rumah ke sekolah (km)"],["kebKhusus","Kebutuhan khusus"],["lingkar","Lingkar kepala"],["layakPip","Layak PIP (usulan sekolah)"],["alasanPip","Alasan layak PIP"],["bank","Bank"],["noRek","Nomor rekening"],["rekNama","Rekening atas nama"],["lintang","Lintang"],["bujur","Bujur"]]],
["Mutasi dan perkembangan",[["status","Status siswa","sel",["Aktif","Pindah","Lulus","Keluar"]],["tglMutasi","Tanggal pindah / lulus / keluar","date"],["sekolahTujuan","Sekolah tujuan atau lanjutan"],["alasan","Alasan mutasi"],["tglMasuk","Tanggal diterima","date"],["sekolahAsal","Sekolah asal"],["tinggi","Tinggi badan (cm)"],["berat","Berat badan (kg)"],["kesehatan","Catatan kesehatan"],["prestasi","Prestasi"],["catatan","Catatan wali kelas"]]]];
function seed(){return SEED.rows.map(function(r,i){var o={id:"s"+i};KEYS.forEach(function(k,j){o[k]=r[j]});return o})}
function load(){try{var r=localStorage.getItem(KEY);data=r?JSON.parse(r):seed()}catch(e){data=seed()}if(!Array.isArray(data))data=seed()}
function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(data))}catch(e){}}
function esc(s){return String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return{"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]})}
var BL=["Januari","Februari","Maret","April","Mei","Juni","Juli","Agustus","September","Oktober","November","Desember"];
function fd(d){var p=(d||"").split("-");return p.length==3?(+p[2])+" "+BL[+p[1]-1]+" "+p[0]:(d||"-")}
function kls(x){return(x.rombel||"").split(" ")[0]}
function filtered(){var q=$("q").value.trim().toLowerCase(),k=$("fk").value,j=$("fj").value;
return data.filter(function(x){return(!k||(k.length==1?kls(x)===k:x.rombel===k))&&(!j||x.jk===j)&&(!q||(x.nama+" "+x.nipd+" "+x.nisn+" "+x.nik).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1)}).sort(function(a,b){return a.nama.localeCompare(b.nama)})}
function render(){
var cur=$("fk").value,rb=Array.from(new Set(data.map(function(x){return x.rombel}).filter(Boolean))).sort(),ks=Array.from(new Set(rb.map(function(r){return r.split(" ")[0]}))).sort();
$("fk").innerHTML='<option value="">Semua kelas</option>'+ks.map(function(k){return'<option value="'+esc(k)+'">Kelas '+esc(k)+' (semua rombel)</option>'}).join("")+rb.map(function(r){return'<option>'+esc(r)+'</option>'}).join("");$("fk").value=cur;
var l=data.filter(function(x){return x.jk==="L"}).length;
var st=[[data.length,"Total siswa"],[l,"Laki-laki"],[data.length-l,"Perempuan"]].concat(ks.map(function(k){return[data.filter(function(x){return kls(x)===k}).length,"Kelas "+k]}));
$("stats").innerHTML=st.map(function(s){return'<div class="stat"><b>'+s[0]+'</b><span>'+s[1]+'</span></div>'}).join("");
var r=filtered(),pages=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(r.length/PS));if(page>=pages)page=pages-1;
var sl=r.slice(page*PS,page*PS+PS);
$("rows").innerHTML=sl.map(function(x,i){return'<tr tabindex="0" data-id="'+x.id+'"><td>'+(page*PS+i+1)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.nama)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.nipd)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.nisn)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.jk)+'</td><td>'+fd(x.tgl)+'</td><td><span class="chip">'+esc(x.rombel||"-")+'</span></td></tr>'}).join("");
$("empty").hidden=r.length>0;$("empty").textContent=data.length?"Tidak ada siswa yang cocok dengan pencarian atau filter.":"Belum ada data. Pilih Tambah siswa atau Kembalikan data awal.";
$("info").textContent=r.length?("Menampilkan "+(page*PS+1)+"–"+(page*PS+sl.length)+" dari "+r.length+" siswa"):"";
$("prev").disabled=page===0;$("next").disabled=page>=pages-1;rekap()}
function rekap(){if(!$("vDash").hidden)dash();if(!$("vStat").hidden)extra("vStat");if(!$("vMut").hidden)extra("vMut");var by={};data.forEach(function(x){var r=x.rombel||"(tanpa rombel)";if(!by[r])by[r]={L:0,P:0};by[r][x.jk==="P"?"P":"L"]++});
var rb=Object.keys(by).sort(),ks=Array.from(new Set(rb.map(function(r){return r.split(" ")[0]}))),h="",gl=0,gp=0;
function row(c,l,p,cls,r){return'<tr'+(cls?' class="'+cls+'"':'')+(r?' data-r="'+esc(r)+'" style="cursor:pointer"':'')+'><td>'+c+'</td><td class="n">'+l+'</td><td class="n">'+p+'</td><td class="n">'+(l+p)+'</td></tr>'}
ks.forEach(function(k){var sl=0,sp=0;rb.filter(function(r){return r.split(" ")[0]===k}).forEach(function(r){h+=row(esc(r),by[r].L,by[r].P,"",r);sl+=by[r].L;sp+=by[r].P});h+=row("Jumlah kelas "+esc(k),sl,sp,"sub");gl+=sl;gp+=sp});
h+=row("Jumlah seluruh siswa",gl,gp,"tot");$("rekapRows").innerHTML=h}
function tab(id){var T={vDash:"DASHBOARD",vData:"DATA SISWA",vRekap:"JUMLAH SISWA",vStat:"STATISTIK",vMut:"MUTASI SISWA",vCad:"CADANGAN DATA",vProf:"PROFIL",vGtk:"TENAGA PENDIDIK",vGtkp:"TENAGA KEPENDIDIKAN"};["vDash","vData","vRekap","vStat","vMut","vCad","vProf","vGtk","vGtkp"].forEach(function(v){$(v).hidden=v!==id});document.body.dataset.tab=id;$("pgTitle").textContent=T[id];if(id==="vDash")dash();if(id==="vProf")prof();if(id==="vGtk"||id==="vGtkp")grows(id);if(id==="vStat"||id==="vMut"||id==="vCad")extra(id);document.querySelectorAll("[data-tab]").forEach(function(b){b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",b.dataset.tab===id)})}
function openForm(id){editId=id;var x=id?data.filter(function(d){return d.id===id})[0]:{jk:"L",agama:"Islam",kec:"Kec. Karang Tengah"};
$("ft").textContent=id?"Ubah data siswa":"Tambah siswa";
$("fb").innerHTML=S.map(function(s){return'<fieldset><legend>'+s[0]+'</legend><div class="grid">'+s[1].map(function(f){var v=esc(x[f[0]]||""),i='f_'+f[0],req=f[0]==="nama"?" required":"";
var inp=f[2]==="sel"?'<select id="'+i+'">'+f[3].map(function(o){return'<option'+(o===x[f[0]]?" selected":"")+'>'+o+'</option>'}).join("")+'</select>':'<input id="'+i+'" type="'+(f[2]||"text")+'" value="'+v+'"'+req+'>';
return'<div><label for="'+i+'">'+f[1]+(req?" *":"")+'</label>'+inp+'</div>'}).join("")+'</div></fieldset>'}).join("");
$("dForm").showModal()}
function openView(id){var x=data.filter(function(d){return d.id===id})[0];if(!x)return;viewId=id;$("vt").textContent=x.nama;
$("vb").innerHTML=S.map(function(s){return'<h3 class="sec">'+s[0]+'</h3><dl class="kv">'+s[1].map(function(f){var v=x[f[0]];if(f[0]==="tgl")v=fd(v);return'<dt>'+f[1]+'</dt><dd>'+esc(v||"-")+'</dd>'}).join("")+'</dl>'}).join("");
$("dView").showModal()}
$("add").onclick=function(){openForm(null)};
$("f").onsubmit=function(e){e.preventDefault();if(!$("f").reportValidity())return false;
var o={};KEYS.forEach(function(k){var el=$("f_"+k);o[k]=el?el.value.trim():""});
if(editId){var old=data.filter(function(d){return d.id===editId})[0];data=data.map(function(d){return d.id===editId?Object.assign({},old,o):d})}else{o.id="n"+Date.now();data.push(o)}
save();$("dForm").close();render();return false};
$("rows").onclick=function(e){var t=e.target.closest("tr");if(t)openView(t.dataset.id)};
$("rows").onkeydown=function(e){if(e.key==="Enter"){var t=e.target.closest("tr");if(t)openView(t.dataset.id)}};
$("vedit").onclick=function(){$("dView").close();openForm(viewId)};
$("vdel").onclick=function(){var x=data.filter(function(d){return d.id===viewId})[0];if(x)ask("Hapus data "+x.nama+"? Tindakan ini tidak bisa dibatalkan.",function(){data=data.filter(function(d){return d.id!==viewId});save();$("dView").close();render();say("Data siswa dihapus.")})};
$("reset").onclick=function(){ask("Kembalikan semua data ke versi unduhan Dapodik? Perubahan yang sudah Anda buat akan hilang.",function(){data=seed();save();page=0;render();say("Data dikembalikan ke versi awal.")})};
$("csv").onclick=function(){var rows=[KEYS].concat(filtered().map(function(x){return KEYS.map(function(k){return x[k]||""})}));
$("csvText").value=rows.map(function(r){return r.map(function(c){return'"'+String(c).replace(/"/g,'""')+'"'}).join(",")}).join("\n");$("dCsv").showModal();$("csvText").select()};
document.querySelectorAll("[data-close]").forEach(function(b){b.onclick=function(){b.closest("dialog").close()}});
$("prev").onclick=function(){page--;render()};$("next").onclick=function(){page++;render()};
["q","fk","fj"].forEach(function(i){$(i).oninput=function(){page=0;render()};$(i).onchange=function(){page=0;render()}});
document.querySelectorAll("[data-tab]").forEach(function(b){b.onclick=function(){tab(b.dataset.tab)}});
$("rekapRows").onclick=function(e){var t=e.target.closest("tr[data-r]");if(t){$("q").value="";$("fj").value="";$("fk").value=t.dataset.r;page=0;render();tab("vData")}};
var M={nama:1,nipd:2,jk:3,nisn:4,tl:5,tgl:6,nik:7,agama:8,alamat:9,rt:10,rw:11,dusun:12,kel:13,kec:14,pos:15,tinggal:16,transport:17,telp:18,hp:19,email:20,kps:22,noKps:23,rombel:42,noUN:43,ijazah:44,kip:45,noKip:46,namaKip:47,kks:48,akta:49,bank:50,noRek:51,rekNama:52,layakPip:53,alasanPip:54,kebKhusus:55,sekolahAsal:56,anakKe:57,lintang:58,bujur:59,noKK:60,berat:61,tinggi:62,lingkar:63,saudara:64,jarak:65};
["ay","ib","wl"].forEach(function(p,i){["Nama","Th","Pend","Kerja","Hasil","Nik"].forEach(function(s,j){M[p+s]=24+i*6+j})});
function cv(v,k){if(v==null)return"";if(k==="tgl"&&typeof v==="number"&&v>20000)return new Date(Math.round((v-25569)*864e5)).toISOString().slice(0,10);var s=String(v).trim();if(/^\d+\.0$/.test(s))s=s.slice(0,-2);return s.split("â€“").join("–")}
function impor(file){if(!window.XLSX){say("Pembaca Excel belum termuat. Periksa koneksi internet lalu muat ulang halaman.");return}
var fr=new FileReader();fr.onload=function(){try{var wb=XLSX.read(fr.result,{type:"array"}),rows=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]],{header:1,raw:true,defval:""}),inc=[];
rows.forEach(function(r){if(typeof r[0]==="number"&&String(r[1]).trim()){var o={};Object.keys(M).forEach(function(k){o[k]=cv(r[M[k]],k)});inc.push(o)}});
if(!inc.length){say("Format file tidak dikenali. Gunakan file daftar peserta didik hasil unduhan Dapodik.");return}
var ix={nisn:{},nik:{},nipd:{}};data.forEach(function(x){["nisn","nik","nipd"].forEach(function(k){if(x[k])ix[k][x[k]]=x})});
var nw=[],ps=[],up=0;inc.forEach(function(o){var ex=(o.nisn&&ix.nisn[o.nisn])||(o.nik&&ix.nik[o.nik])||(o.nipd&&ix.nipd[o.nipd]);if(ex)ps.push([ex,o]);else nw.push(o)});
ps.forEach(function(p){if(Object.keys(M).some(function(k){return !p[0][k]&&p[1][k]}))up++});
ask("File berisi "+inc.length+" siswa: "+nw.length+" siswa baru akan ditambahkan, "+ps.length+" sudah ada di aplikasi ("+up+" di antaranya akan dilengkapi kolom yang masih kosong; data yang sudah terisi tidak diubah). Lanjutkan?",function(){
nw.forEach(function(o,i){o.id="i"+Date.now()+"_"+i;data.push(o)});
ps.forEach(function(p){Object.keys(M).forEach(function(k){if(!p[0][k]&&p[1][k])p[0][k]=p[1][k]})});
save();page=0;render();say("Impor selesai: "+nw.length+" siswa baru, "+up+" data dilengkapi.")})}catch(e){say("File tidak bisa dibaca. Pastikan formatnya .xlsx hasil unduhan Dapodik.")}};fr.readAsArrayBuffer(file)}
$("impBtn").onclick=function(){$("impFile").click()};$("impFile").onchange=function(){var f=this.files[0];if(f)impor(f);this.value=""};
var SET={school:"SD NEGERI KARANG TENGAH 12",area:"Kec. Karang Tengah, Kota Tangerang, Prov. Banten",user:"",hash:""},SK="buku-induk-setelan-v1";
try{var sv=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(SK));if(sv)SET=Object.assign(SET,sv)}catch(e){}
function saveSet(){try{localStorage.setItem(SK,JSON.stringify(SET))}catch(e){}}
function applySet(){$("hSchool").textContent=SET.school;$("hArea").textContent=SET.area;document.title="Buku Induk Siswa – "+SET.school;$("sbSchool").textContent=SET.school;$("ftSchool").textContent=SET.school;$("hiAv").innerHTML=SET.foto?'<img src="'+SET.foto+'" alt="Foto profil">':esc((SET.operator||SET.user||"A").charAt(0).toUpperCase())}
function sha(s){var b=new TextEncoder().encode("bi:"+s);if(window.crypto&&crypto.subtle)return crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256",b).then(function(h){return Array.from(new Uint8Array(h)).map(function(x){return("0"+x.toString(16)).slice(-2)}).join("")});var h=5381;for(var i=0;i<b.length;i++)h=((h<<5)+h+b[i])|0;return Promise.resolve("w"+h)}
function lock(){var su=!SET.hash;$("login").hidden=false;document.body.classList.add("locked");$("lSchool").textContent=su?"Selamat datang":SET.school;$("lSub").textContent=su?"Atur nama sekolah dan akun pengelola untuk memulai.":"Masuk untuk membuka buku induk siswa.";document.querySelectorAll(".su").forEach(function(e){e.hidden=!su});$("lGo").textContent=su?"Simpan dan masuk":"Masuk";$("lForgot").hidden=su;$("lMsg").textContent="";$("lSch").value=SET.school;$("lAre").value=SET.area;$("lPass").value=""}
function unlock(){try{sessionStorage.setItem("bi-auth","1")}catch(e){}$("login").hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove("locked");$("lPass").value="";$("lPass2").value=""}
$("lf").onsubmit=function(e){e.preventDefault();var u=$("lUser").value.trim(),p=$("lPass").value,m=$("lMsg");
if(!SET.hash){if(!$("lSch").value.trim()){m.textContent="Isi nama sekolah.";return false}if(!u){m.textContent="Isi nama pengguna.";return false}if(p.length<4){m.textContent="Kata sandi minimal 4 karakter.";return false}if(p!==$("lPass2").value){m.textContent="Kata sandi dan ulangi kata sandi harus sama.";return false}
sha(p).then(function(h){SET.school=$("lSch").value.trim();SET.area=$("lAre").value.trim();SET.user=u;SET.hash=h;saveSet();applySet();unlock();say("Akun dibuat. Selamat datang.")})}
else{sha(p).then(function(h){if(u.toLowerCase()===SET.user.toLowerCase()&&h===SET.hash)unlock();else m.textContent="Nama pengguna atau kata sandi salah."})}return false};
$("lForgot").onclick=function(){ask("Akun pengelola (nama pengguna dan kata sandi) akan dihapus, lalu Anda diminta membuat akun baru. Data siswa, guru, dan tendik TIDAK dihapus. Lanjutkan?",function(){SET.hash="";SET.user="";saveSet();try{sessionStorage.removeItem("bi-auth")}catch(e){}$("lUser").value="";lock();say("Akun dihapus. Silakan buat akun baru.")})};
$("outBtn").onclick=function(){try{sessionStorage.removeItem("bi-auth")}catch(e){}$("lUser").value="";lock()};
$("setBtn").onclick=function(){$("sSch").value=SET.school;$("sAre").value=SET.area;$("sUser").value=SET.user;$("sNew").value="";$("dSet").showModal()};
$("sSave").onclick=function(){var s=$("sSch").value.trim(),u=$("sUser").value.trim(),n=$("sNew").value;if(!s||!u){say("Nama sekolah dan nama pengguna wajib diisi.");return}if(n&&n.length<4){say("Kata sandi baru minimal 4 karakter.");return}
function done(h){SET.school=s;SET.area=$("sAre").value.trim();SET.user=u;if(h)SET.hash=h;saveSet();applySet();$("dSet").close();say("Pengaturan tersimpan.")}
if(n)sha(n).then(done);else done()};
applySet();var au=false;try{au=sessionStorage.getItem("bi-auth")==="1"}catch(e){}if(au&&SET.hash){$("login").hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove("locked")}else lock();
var tt;function say(m){var t=$("toast");t.textContent=m;t.style.display="block";clearTimeout(tt);tt=setTimeout(function(){t.style.display="none"},3500)}
var yes=null;function ask(m,cb){yes=cb;$("askMsg").textContent=m;$("dAsk").showModal()}
$("askYes").onclick=function(){$("dAsk").close();var f=yes;yes=null;if(f)f()};$("askNo").onclick=function(){$("dAsk").close();yes=null};
function ic(c){return'<svg width="34" height="34" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="'+c+'" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4.5"/><path d="M3.5 21c0-4.7 3.8-7.5 8.5-7.5s8.5 2.8 8.5 7.5z"/></svg>'}
function dash(){var ks=Array.from(new Set(data.map(kls).filter(Boolean))).sort(),L=[],P=[];
ks.forEach(function(k){var a=data.filter(function(x){return kls(x)===k});L.push(a.filter(function(x){return x.jk==="L"}).length);P.push(a.length-L[L.length-1])});
var mx=Math.max.apply(null,L.map(function(l,i){return l+P[i]}).concat([1])),step=Math.max(10,Math.ceil(mx/4/10)*10),top=step*4,W=600,H=300,ml=44,mb=38,mt=14,pw=W-ml-10,ph=H-mt-mb,g=pw/Math.max(ks.length,1),bw=Math.min(26,g/4),s='<svg viewBox="0 0 '+W+' '+H+'" width="100%" role="img" aria-label="Grafik jumlah siswa per kelas">';
for(var i=0;i<=4;i++){var y=mt+ph-ph*i/4;s+='<line x1="'+ml+'" x2="'+(W-10)+'" y1="'+y+'" y2="'+y+'" stroke="#9aa6bb" stroke-opacity=".4" stroke-dasharray="3 3"/><text x="'+(ml-8)+'" y="'+(y+4)+'" font-size="11" text-anchor="end" fill="#7a869a">'+step*i+'</text>'}
ks.forEach(function(k,i){var cx=ml+g*i+g/2,v=[L[i],P[i],L[i]+P[i]],c=["#7c4dff","#f0357c","#ffa800"];v.forEach(function(n,j){var h=ph*n/top,x=cx+(j-1)*(bw+4)-bw/2;s+='<rect x="'+x+'" y="'+(mt+ph-h)+'" width="'+bw+'" height="'+h+'" rx="5" fill="'+c[j]+'"><title>'+["Laki-laki","Perempuan","Jumlah"][j]+": "+n+'</title></rect>'});s+='<text x="'+cx+'" y="'+(H-14)+'" font-size="12" text-anchor="middle" fill="#7a869a">Kelas '+esc(k)+'</text>'});
s+='</svg>';
var st=cnt(function(x){return x.status||"Aktif"}),lk=data.filter(function(x){return x.jk==="L"}).length;
var sm=[["Aktif",st.Aktif||0,"#7c4dff"],["Lulus",st.Lulus||0,"#1fb5a5"],["Pindah",st.Pindah||0,"#ffa800"],["Non aktif",st.Keluar||0,"#f0357c"]];
$("vDash").innerHTML='<div class="dgrid"><div class="dcard"><h3>Jumlah peserta didik per kelas</h3>'+s+'<div class="legend"><span><i style="background:#7c4dff"></i>Laki-laki</span><span><i style="background:#f0357c"></i>Perempuan</span><span><i style="background:#ffa800"></i>Jumlah</span></div></div><div><div class="two"><div class="big b1">'+ic("#fff")+'<div><b>'+lk+'</b><span>Siswa laki-laki</span></div></div><div class="big b2">'+ic("#fff")+'<div><b>'+(data.length-lk)+'</b><span>Siswa perempuan</span></div></div></div><div class="four">'+sm.map(function(m){return'<div class="sm">'+ic(m[2])+'<b>'+m[1]+'</b><span style="color:'+m[2]+'">'+m[0]+'</span></div>'}).join("")+'</div></div></div>'}
function xls(){var mv=data.filter(function(x){return x.status&&x.status!=="Aktif"});
if(!mv.length){say("Belum ada data mutasi untuk diunduh.");return}
if(!window.XLSX||!window.claude||!claude.use){say("Unduhan Excel tidak tersedia di tampilan ini.");return}
claude.use("downloads").then(function(dl){if(!dl){say("Unduhan tidak tersedia di tampilan ini.");return}
var ST=["Pindah","Lulus","Keluar"],wb=XLSX.utils.book_new(),H=["No","Nama","NIPD","NISN","L/P","Tempat lahir","Tanggal lahir","Rombel","Tanggal mutasi","Sekolah tujuan atau lanjutan","Alasan"];
var rk=[["Rekap Mutasi Siswa"],[SET.school],["Tanggal unduh: "+fd(new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10))],[],["Status","Laki-laki","Perempuan","Jumlah"]],tl=0,tp=0;
ST.forEach(function(s){var a=mv.filter(function(x){return x.status===s}),l=a.filter(function(x){return x.jk==="L"}).length;rk.push([s,l,a.length-l,a.length]);tl+=l;tp+=a.length-l});
rk.push(["Jumlah",tl,tp,tl+tp],[],["Rombel","Pindah","Lulus","Keluar","Jumlah"]);
Array.from(new Set(mv.map(function(x){return x.rombel||"-"}))).sort().forEach(function(r){var c=ST.map(function(s){return mv.filter(function(x){return(x.rombel||"-")===r&&x.status===s}).length});rk.push([r].concat(c,[c[0]+c[1]+c[2]]))});
var w0=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(rk);w0["!cols"]=[{wch:24},{wch:12},{wch:12},{wch:10},{wch:10}];XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,w0,"Rekap");
ST.forEach(function(s){var rows=[H].concat(mv.filter(function(x){return x.status===s}).sort(function(a,b){return a.nama.localeCompare(b.nama)}).map(function(x,i){return[i+1,x.nama,x.nipd,x.nisn,x.jk,x.tl,fd(x.tgl),x.rombel,x.tglMutasi?fd(x.tglMutasi):"",x.sekolahTujuan||"",x.alasan||""]}));var w=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(rows);w["!cols"]=[{wch:5},{wch:34},{wch:12},{wch:13},{wch:5},{wch:16},{wch:18},{wch:8},{wch:18},{wch:30},{wch:28}];XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,w,s)});
var out=XLSX.write(wb,{bookType:"xlsx",type:"array"});
dl.save({filename:"rekap-mutasi-siswa-"+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+".xlsx",data:new Blob([out])}).then(function(){say("Berkas Excel disimpan.")}).catch(function(er){if(!er||er.code!=="declined")say("Unduhan gagal: "+((er&&er.message)||"berkas tidak dapat disimpan")+".")})})}
function prof(){var s=SET,n=new Set(data.map(function(x){return x.rombel}).filter(Boolean)).size;
function f(id,l,v,t,full){return'<div'+(full?' style="grid-column:1/-1"':'')+'><label for="'+id+'">'+l+'</label><input id="'+id+'" type="'+(t||"text")+'" value="'+esc(v||"")+'"></div>'}
$("vProf").innerHTML='<div class="pgrid"><div class="dcard pc"><div class="bigav">'+(s.foto?'<img src="'+s.foto+'" alt="Foto profil">':esc((s.operator||s.user||"A").charAt(0).toUpperCase()))+'</div><div class="fbtn"><button type="button" id="pFotoBtn">Ganti foto</button>'+(s.foto?'<button type="button" id="pFotoDel">Hapus foto</button>':"")+'</div><input type="file" id="pFotoFile" accept="image/*" hidden><h3>'+esc(s.operator||s.user||"Pengelola")+'</h3><p class="sub">Operator '+esc(s.school)+'</p><div class="pst"><div><b>'+data.length+'</b><span>Siswa</span></div><div><b>'+n+'</b><span>Rombel</span></div></div><dl class="kv"><dt>Nama pengguna</dt><dd>'+esc(s.user)+'</dd><dt>NPSN</dt><dd>'+esc(s.npsn||"-")+'</dd><dt>Kepala sekolah</dt><dd>'+esc(s.kepsek||"-")+'</dd><dt>Tahun ajaran</dt><dd>'+esc(s.ta||"-")+'</dd><dt>Akreditasi</dt><dd>'+esc(s.akr||"-")+'</dd></dl></div><div><div class="dcard" style="margin-bottom:16px"><h3>Profil sekolah</h3><div class="grid">'+f("pSch","Nama sekolah",s.school,"",1)+f("pNpsn","NPSN",s.npsn)+f("pAkr","Akreditasi",s.akr)+f("pAre","Alamat atau wilayah",s.area,"",1)+f("pKep","Kepala sekolah",s.kepsek)+f("pNip","NIP kepala sekolah",s.nip)+f("pTa","Tahun ajaran",s.ta)+f("pTel","Telepon",s.tel,"tel")+f("pEml","E-mail",s.eml,"email",1)+f("pOp","Nama operator",s.operator)+f("pUser","Nama pengguna",s.user)+'</div><p style="margin:14px 0 0"><button class="pri" id="pSave">Simpan profil</button></p></div><div class="dcard"><h3>Ubah kata sandi</h3><div class="grid">'+f("pOld","Kata sandi lama","","password",1)+f("pNew","Kata sandi baru","","password")+f("pNew2","Ulangi kata sandi baru","","password")+'</div><p style="margin:14px 0 0"><button id="pPass">Ubah kata sandi</button></p></div></div></div>'}
$("vProf").onchange=function(e){if(e.target.id!=="pFotoFile")return;var fl=e.target.files&&e.target.files[0];if(!fl)return;if(!/^image\//.test(fl.type)){say("Berkas harus berupa gambar.");return}
var rd=new FileReader();rd.onload=function(){var im=new Image();im.onload=function(){var z=256,c=document.createElement("canvas");c.width=c.height=z;var m=Math.min(im.width,im.height),sx=(im.width-m)/2,sy=(im.height-m)/2;c.getContext("2d").drawImage(im,sx,sy,m,m,0,0,z,z);SET.foto=c.toDataURL("image/jpeg",0.85);saveSet();applySet();prof();say("Foto profil diganti.")};im.onerror=function(){say("Gambar tidak dapat dibaca.")};im.src=rd.result};rd.readAsDataURL(fl)};
$("vProf").onclick=function(e){
if(e.target.id==="pFotoBtn"){$("pFotoFile").click();return}
if(e.target.id==="pFotoDel"){delete SET.foto;saveSet();applySet();prof();say("Foto profil dihapus.");return}
if(e.target.id==="pSave"){var sc=$("pSch").value.trim(),us=$("pUser").value.trim();if(!sc||!us){say("Nama sekolah dan nama pengguna wajib diisi.");return}
SET.school=sc;SET.npsn=$("pNpsn").value.trim();SET.akr=$("pAkr").value.trim();SET.area=$("pAre").value.trim();SET.kepsek=$("pKep").value.trim();SET.nip=$("pNip").value.trim();SET.ta=$("pTa").value.trim();SET.tel=$("pTel").value.trim();SET.eml=$("pEml").value.trim();SET.operator=$("pOp").value.trim();SET.user=us;saveSet();applySet();prof();say("Profil tersimpan.")}
if(e.target.id==="pPass"){var nw=$("pNew").value;if(nw.length<4){say("Kata sandi baru minimal 4 karakter.");return}if(nw!==$("pNew2").value){say("Ulangi kata sandi baru dengan isian yang sama.");return}
sha($("pOld").value).then(function(h){if(h!==SET.hash){say("Kata sandi lama salah.");return}sha(nw).then(function(h2){SET.hash=h2;saveSet();$("pOld").value="";$("pNew").value="";$("pNew2").value="";say("Kata sandi diganti.")})})}};

var GI=null,GKind="pendidik";
function gbuild(id){var c=$(id);if(c.dataset.b)return;c.dataset.b=1;var k=id==="vGtk"?"pendidik":"kependidikan";
c.innerHTML='<div class="stats gst"></div><div class="bar"><input type="search" placeholder="Cari nama, NIP, NUPTK, atau NIK" aria-label="Cari"><button class="pri" type="button">Tambah</button> <button type="button" class="gimp">Tambah dari Excel</button><input type="file" class="gfile" accept=".xlsx,.xls" hidden></div><div class="tbl"><table><thead><tr><th>No</th><th>Nama</th><th>NIP atau NUPTK</th><th>L/P</th><th>'+(k==="pendidik"?"Pangkat / gol.":"Jabatan / tugas")+'</th><th>Status</th><th>Telepon</th></tr></thead><tbody></tbody></table><div class="empty" hidden></div></div>';
c.querySelector("input").oninput=function(){grows(id)};
c.querySelector("button").onclick=function(){gform(k,null)};
c.querySelector(".gimp").onclick=function(){c.querySelector(".gfile").click()};
c.querySelector(".gfile").onchange=function(){var f=this.files[0];this.value="";if(f)gimpor(f,k,id)};
c.querySelector("tbody").onclick=function(e){var t=e.target.closest("tr[data-i]");if(t)gform(k,+t.dataset.i)}}
function grows(id){gbuild(id);var k=id==="vGtk"?"pendidik":"kependidikan",c=$(id),g=GT[k],q=c.querySelector("input").value.trim().toLowerCase();
c.querySelector(".gst").innerHTML=[[g.length,"Total"],[g.filter(function(x){return x.jk==="L"}).length,"Laki-laki"],[g.filter(function(x){return x.jk!=="L"}).length,"Perempuan"]].map(function(s){return'<div class="stat"><b>'+s[0]+'</b><span>'+s[1]+'</span></div>'}).join("");
var L=g.map(function(x,i){return{x:x,i:i}}).filter(function(o){return !q||((o.x.nama||"")+" "+(o.x.nip||"")+" "+(o.x.nuptk||"")+" "+(o.x.nik||"")).toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>-1}).sort(function(a,b){return(a.x.nama||"").localeCompare(b.x.nama||"")}),f=k==="pendidik"?"pangkat":"jabatan";
c.querySelector("tbody").innerHTML=L.map(function(o,n){var x=o.x;return'<tr data-i="'+o.i+'"><td>'+(n+1)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.nama)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.nip||"-")+'</td><td>'+esc(x.jk||"-")+'</td><td>'+esc(x[f]||(k==="pendidik"?"":x.tugas)||"-")+'</td><td><span class="chip">'+esc(x.status||"-")+'</span></td><td>'+esc(x.telp||x.hp||"-")+'</td></tr>'}).join("");
var em=c.querySelector(".empty");em.hidden=L.length>0;em.textContent=g.length?"Tidak ada data yang cocok.":"Belum ada data. Pilih Tambah untuk mengisi."}
function gform(k,i){GI=i;GKind=k;var x=i==null?{jk:"L",status:"PNS"}:GT[k][i];
$("gtkT").textContent=(i==null?"Tambah ":"Ubah data ")+(k==="pendidik"?"tenaga pendidik":"tenaga kependidikan");
var inp=function(f){var id="g_"+f[0],v=esc(x[f[0]]||""),req=f[0]==="nama"?" required":"",lab='<label for="'+id+'">'+f[1]+(req?" *":"")+'</label>',w=f[2]==="area"?'<div style="grid-column:1/-1">':'<div>';
if(f[2]==="sel"){var ops=f[3].slice();if(x[f[0]]&&ops.indexOf(x[f[0]])<0)ops.push(x[f[0]]);return w+lab+'<select id="'+id+'">'+ops.map(function(o){return'<option'+(o===x[f[0]]?" selected":"")+'>'+o+'</option>'}).join("")+'</select></div>'}
if(f[2]==="area")return w+lab+'<textarea id="'+id+'" rows="2">'+v+'</textarea></div>';
return w+lab+'<input id="'+id+'" type="'+f[2]+'" value="'+v+'"'+req+'></div>'};
$("gtkB").innerHTML='<div class="grid">'+gall(k).map(inp).join("")+'</div>';
$("gtkDel").hidden=i==null;$("dGtk").showModal()}
$("gtkF").onsubmit=function(e){e.preventDefault();if(!$("gtkF").reportValidity())return false;var o={};gall(GKind).forEach(function(f){o[f[0]]=$("g_"+f[0]).value.trim()});if(GI==null)GT[GKind].push(o);else GT[GKind][GI]=Object.assign({},GT[GKind][GI],o);gsave();$("dGtk").close();grows(GKind==="pendidik"?"vGtk":"vGtkp");say(GI==null?"Data ditambahkan.":"Data tersimpan.");return false};
$("gtkDel").onclick=function(){var x=GT[GKind][GI];if(!x)return;ask("Hapus data "+x.nama+"? Tindakan ini tidak bisa dibatalkan.",function(){GT[GKind].splice(GI,1);gsave();$("dGtk").close();grows(GKind==="pendidik"?"vGtk":"vGtkp");say("Data dihapus.")})};

var GH={"Nama":"nama","NUPTK":"nuptk","JK":"jk","Tempat Lahir":"tl","Tanggal Lahir":"tgl","NIP":"nip","Status Kepegawaian":"status","Jenis PTK":"jenis","Agama":"agama","Alamat Jalan":"alamat","RT":"rt","RW":"rw","Nama Dusun":"dusun","Desa/Kelurahan":"desa","Kecamatan":"kec","Kode Pos":"pos","Telepon":"telp","HP":"hp","Email":"email","Tugas Tambahan":"tugas","SK CPNS":"skCpns","Tanggal CPNS":"tglCpns","SK Pengangkatan":"skAng","TMT Pengangkatan":"tmt","Lembaga Pengangkatan":"lembaga","Pangkat Golongan":"pangkat","Sumber Gaji":"gaji","Nama Ibu Kandung":"ibu","Status Perkawinan":"kawin","Nama Suami/Istri":"pasangan","NIP Suami/Istri":"nipPasangan","Pekerjaan Suami/Istri":"kerjaPasangan","TMT PNS":"tmtPns","NPWP":"npwp","Bank":"bank","Nomor Rekening Bank":"rek","Rekening Atas Nama":"rekNama","NIK":"nik","No KK":"kk","Karpeg":"karpeg","Karis/Karsu":"karis"};
var GDT={tgl:1,tglCpns:1,tmt:1,tmtPns:1};
function gcv(v,key){if(v==null)return"";if(GDT[key]&&typeof v==="number"&&v>20000)return new Date(Math.round((v-25569)*864e5)).toISOString().slice(0,10);var t=String(v).trim();if(/^\d+\.0$/.test(t))t=t.slice(0,-2);return t}
function gkeys(x){var a=[];["nik","nip","nuptk"].forEach(function(f){if(x[f])a.push(f+":"+x[f])});if(!a.length&&x.nama)a.push("n:"+x.nama.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g," "));return a}
function gplan(k,inc){var ix={};GT[k].forEach(function(x){gkeys(x).forEach(function(z){ix[z]=x})});var nw=[],ps=[],up=0;
inc.forEach(function(o){var ex=null;gkeys(o).forEach(function(z){if(!ex&&ix[z])ex=ix[z]});if(!ex&&o.nama){var nn="n:"+o.nama.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g," ");GT[k].forEach(function(x){if(!ex&&x.nama&&"n:"+x.nama.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g," ")===nn)ex=x})}
if(ex){ps.push([ex,o]);if(Object.keys(o).some(function(f){return !ex[f]&&o[f]}))up++}else nw.push(o)});return{nw:nw,ps:ps,up:up}}
function gapply(k,pl){pl.nw.forEach(function(o){GT[k].push(o)});pl.ps.forEach(function(p){Object.keys(p[1]).forEach(function(f){if(!p[0][f]&&p[1][f])p[0][f]=p[1][f]})});gsave()}
function gimpor(file,k,id){if(!window.XLSX){say("Pembaca Excel belum termuat. Periksa koneksi internet lalu muat ulang halaman.");return}
var fr=new FileReader();fr.onload=function(){try{var wb=XLSX.read(fr.result,{type:"array"}),ws=wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]],rows=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws,{header:1,raw:true,defval:""}),hi=-1;
rows.forEach(function(r,i){if(hi<0&&r.indexOf("Nama")>-1&&r.indexOf("JK")>-1)hi=i});
if(hi<0){say("Format file tidak dikenali. Gunakan file daftar guru atau daftar tendik hasil unduhan Dapodik.");return}
var ttl=String(rows[0][0]||"").toLowerCase();
if(k==="pendidik"&&ttl.indexOf("kependidikan")>-1||k==="kependidikan"&&ttl.indexOf("guru")>-1){say(k==="pendidik"?"Ini file tenaga kependidikan. Impor lewat tab Tenaga Kependidikan.":"Ini file daftar guru. Impor lewat tab Tenaga Pendidik.");return}
var H=rows[hi],col={};H.forEach(function(h,i){var key=GH[String(h).trim()];if(key&&col[key]==null)col[key]=i});
var inc=[];rows.slice(hi+1).forEach(function(r){if(!String(r[col.nama]==null?"":r[col.nama]).trim())return;var o={};Object.keys(col).forEach(function(f){var v=gcv(r[col[f]],f);if(v)o[f]=v});if(!o.nip&&o.nuptk)o.nip=o.nuptk;
if(k==="kependidikan"){var j=o.jenis==="Kepala Sekolah"?"Kepala Sekolah":(o.tugas||"");if(j)o.jabatan=j}inc.push(o)});
if(!inc.length){say("Tidak ada baris data pada file.");return}
var pl=gplan(k,inc);
ask("File berisi "+inc.length+" orang: "+pl.nw.length+" data baru akan ditambahkan, "+pl.ps.length+" sudah ada ("+pl.up+" di antaranya akan dilengkapi kolom yang masih kosong; data yang sudah terisi tidak diubah). Lanjutkan?",function(){gapply(k,pl);grows(id);say("Impor selesai: "+pl.nw.length+" data baru, "+pl.up+" data dilengkapi.")})}catch(e){say("File tidak bisa dibaca. Pastikan formatnya .xlsx hasil unduhan Dapodik.")}};fr.readAsArrayBuffer(file)}
try{if(!localStorage.getItem("buku-induk-gtk-seed-v1")){["pendidik","kependidikan"].forEach(function(k){gapply(k,gplan(k,GSEED[k].map(function(o){return Object.assign({},o)})))});localStorage.setItem("buku-induk-gtk-seed-v1","1")}}catch(e){if(!GT.pendidik.length&&!GT.kependidikan.length){GT={pendidik:GSEED.pendidik.slice(),kependidikan:GSEED.kependidikan.slice()}}}
function cnt(f){var o={};data.forEach(function(x){var k=f(x)||"Tidak diisi";o[k]=(o[k]||0)+1});return o}
function hist(t,o){var ks=Object.keys(o).sort(function(a,b){return o[b]-o[a]}),m=o[ks[0]]||1;return'<div class="card"><h3>'+t+'</h3>'+ks.map(function(k){return'<div class="hb"><span>'+esc(k)+'</span><i style="width:'+Math.round(o[k]/m*100)+'%"></i><b>'+o[k]+'</b></div>'}).join("")+'</div>'}
function age(x){if(!x.tgl)return"";var d=new Date(x.tgl),n=new Date(),a=n.getFullYear()-d.getFullYear();if(n<new Date(n.getFullYear(),d.getMonth(),d.getDate()))a--;return a+" tahun"}
function extra(id){
if(id==="vStat"){var P=[["Usia",age],["Agama",function(x){return x.agama}],["Penghasilan ayah",function(x){return x.ayHasil}],["Pekerjaan ayah",function(x){return x.ayKerja}],["Penerima KIP",function(x){return x.kip}],["Penerima KPS",function(x){return x.kps}],["Jenis tinggal",function(x){return x.tinggal}],["Alat transportasi",function(x){return x.transport}]];
$("vStat").innerHTML='<div class="cards">'+P.map(function(p){return hist(p[0],cnt(p[1]))}).join("")+'</div>'}
if(id==="vMut"){var st=cnt(function(x){return x.status||"Aktif"}),mv=data.filter(function(x){return x.status&&x.status!=="Aktif"});
$("vMut").innerHTML='<div class="stats">'+["Aktif","Pindah","Lulus","Keluar"].map(function(s){return'<div class="stat"><b>'+(st[s]||0)+'</b><span>'+s+'</span></div>'}).join("")+'</div><div class="card" style="margin:0 0 14px"><h3>Catat mutasi siswa</h3><div class="grid"><div><label for="mSis">Siswa</label><input id="mSis" list="mList" placeholder="Ketik nama atau NIPD, lalu pilih"><datalist id="mList">'+data.map(function(x){return'<option value="'+esc(x.nama+" – "+(x.rombel||"-")+" – "+(x.nipd||x.id))+'">'}).join("")+'</datalist></div><div><label for="mSt">Status</label><select id="mSt"><option>Pindah</option><option>Lulus</option><option>Keluar</option><option>Aktif</option></select></div><div><label for="mTgl">Tanggal</label><input id="mTgl" type="date"></div><div><label for="mTuj">Sekolah tujuan atau lanjutan</label><input id="mTuj"></div><div style="grid-column:1/-1"><label for="mAls">Alasan</label><input id="mAls"></div></div><p style="margin:12px 0 0"><button class="pri" id="mSave">Simpan mutasi</button></p></div><p style="margin:0 0 10px"><button class="pri" id="mXls">Unduh rekap Excel (pindah, lulus, keluar)</button></p><p class="sub">Klik baris pada tabel untuk melihat atau mengubah data lengkap siswa. Pilih status Aktif untuk membatalkan mutasi.</p><div class="tbl"><table><thead><tr><th>Nama</th><th>Rombel</th><th>Status</th><th>Tanggal</th><th>Sekolah tujuan</th><th>Alasan</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+mv.map(function(x){return'<tr tabindex="0" data-id="'+x.id+'"><td>'+esc(x.nama)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.rombel)+'</td><td><span class="chip">'+esc(x.status)+'</span></td><td>'+fd(x.tglMutasi)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.sekolahTujuan||"-")+'</td><td>'+esc(x.alasan||"-")+'</td><td><button type="button" data-undo="'+x.id+'">Jadikan aktif</button></td></tr>'}).join("")+'</tbody></table>'+(mv.length?'':'<div class="empty">Belum ada siswa yang pindah, lulus, atau keluar.</div>')+'</div>'}
if(id==="vCad"){$("bk").value=JSON.stringify(data);$("bk").select()}}
$("vMut").onclick=function(e){if(e.target.id==="mXls"){xls();return}var u=e.target.closest("[data-undo]");if(u){var z=data.filter(function(x){return x.id===u.dataset.undo})[0];if(z)ask("Hapus data mutasi "+z.nama+" dan kembalikan ke siswa aktif?",function(){z.status="Aktif";z.tglMutasi="";z.sekolahTujuan="";z.alasan="";save();render();say(z.nama+" kembali menjadi siswa aktif.")});return}if(e.target.id==="mSave"){var v=$("mSis").value.trim().toLowerCase(),m=data.filter(function(x){return(x.nama+" – "+(x.rombel||"-")+" – "+(x.nipd||x.id)).toLowerCase()===v});if(!m.length)m=data.filter(function(x){return x.nama.toLowerCase()===v});if(m.length!==1){say("Pilih satu siswa dari daftar saran. Ketik nama atau NIPD, lalu pilih dari daftar.");return}var s=$("mSt").value;if(s!=="Aktif"&&!$("mTgl").value){say("Isi tanggal mutasi terlebih dahulu.");return}var o=m[0],a=s==="Aktif";o.status=s;o.tglMutasi=a?"":$("mTgl").value;o.sekolahTujuan=a?"":$("mTuj").value.trim();o.alasan=a?"":$("mAls").value.trim();save();render();say("Mutasi "+o.nama+" tersimpan.");return}var t=e.target.closest("tr[data-id]");if(t)openView(t.dataset.id)};
$("bkRestore").onclick=function(){try{var d=JSON.parse($("bk").value);if(!Array.isArray(d)||!d.every(function(x){return x&&x.id&&x.nama}))throw 0;ask("Pulihkan "+d.length+" siswa dari cadangan ini? Data saat ini akan diganti.",function(){data=d;save();page=0;render();say("Data dipulihkan.")})}catch(e){say("Teks cadangan tidak valid. Pastikan seluruh isi cadangan tertempel utuh.")}};
$("tbOut").onclick=function(){$("outBtn").click()};$("sbSet").onclick=function(){$("setBtn").click()};$("sbImp").onclick=function(){$("impBtn").click()};

var PA=function(){return $("printArea")};
function pv(v){return esc(v||"-")}
function rowsT(a){return'<table>'+a.map(function(r){return'<tr><td>'+r[0]+'</td><td>:</td><td>'+pv(r[1])+'</td></tr>'}).join("")+'</table>'}
function bukuIndukHtml(x,n){var al=[x.alamat,x.rt||x.rw?("RT "+(x.rt||"-")+"/RW "+(x.rw||"-")):"",x.dusun,x.kel,x.kec,x.pos].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
var tgl=fd(new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)),kota=(SET.area||"").split(",")[0].replace(/^Kec\.\s*/,"")||"";
function ortu(p){return rowsT([["Nama",x[p+"Nama"]],["Tahun lahir",x[p+"Th"]&&x[p+"Th"]!=="0"&&x[p+"Th"]!=="1900"?x[p+"Th"]:""],["Pendidikan",x[p+"Pend"]],["Pekerjaan",x[p+"Kerja"]],["Penghasilan",x[p+"Hasil"]],["NIK",x[p+"Nik"]]])}
var hasW=x.wlNama;
return'<section class="pg-bi">'+kopHtml(kopVal(),SET.kopLogo,SET.kopLogoR)+'<h2>BUKU INDUK SISWA</h2><div class="no">No. Induk (NIPD): '+pv(x.nipd)+' · NISN: '+pv(x.nisn)+'</div>'+
'<div class="bitop"><div class="tb"><h4 style="margin-top:0">A. Identitas peserta didik</h4>'+rowsT([["Nama lengkap",x.nama],["Jenis kelamin",x.jk==="L"?"Laki-laki":x.jk==="P"?"Perempuan":""],["Tempat, tanggal lahir",(x.tl||"-")+", "+fd(x.tgl)],["NIK",x.nik],["Agama",x.agama],["No. akta lahir",x.akta],["Rombel saat ini",x.rombel],["Status",x.status||"Aktif"]])+'</div><div class="foto">Foto<br>3 x 4</div></div>'+
'<h4>B. Alamat dan kontak</h4>'+rowsT([["Alamat",al],["Jenis tinggal",x.tinggal],["Alat transportasi",x.transport],["Telepon / HP",[x.telp,x.hp].filter(Boolean).join(" / ")],["E-mail",x.email]])+
'<h4>C. Data orang tua dan wali</h4><div class="duo'+(hasW?' duo3':'')+'"><div><b>Ayah</b>'+ortu("ay")+'</div><div><b>Ibu</b>'+ortu("ib")+'</div>'+(hasW?'<div><b>Wali</b>'+ortu("wl")+'</div>':'')+'</div>'+
'<h4>D. Sekolah dan bantuan</h4>'+rowsT([["Tanggal diterima",x.tglMasuk?fd(x.tglMasuk):""],["Sekolah asal",x.sekolahAsal],["Penerima KPS",x.kps],["Penerima KIP",x.kip],["No. KIP",x.noKip]])+
'<h4>E. Perkembangan siswa</h4>'+rowsT([["Tinggi / berat badan",(x.tinggi||"-")+" cm / "+(x.berat||"-")+" kg"],["Catatan kesehatan",x.kesehatan],["Prestasi",x.prestasi],["Catatan wali kelas",x.catatan]])+
(x.status&&x.status!=="Aktif"?'<h4>F. Mutasi</h4>'+rowsT([["Status",x.status],["Tanggal",x.tglMutasi?fd(x.tglMutasi):""],["Sekolah tujuan",x.sekolahTujuan],["Alasan",x.alasan]]):"")+
'<div class="ttd"><div>Orang tua / wali<div class="sp"></div>(.............................)</div><div>'+esc(kota)+', '+tgl+'<br>Kepala Sekolah<div class="sp"></div><b><u>'+esc(SET.kepsek||".............................")+'</u></b><br>'+(SET.nip?'NIP. '+esc(SET.nip):'')+'</div></div></section>'}
function kartuHtml(x){return'<div class="kartu"><div class="kh"><b>KARTU PESERTA DIDIK</b><span>'+esc(SET.school||"")+'</span></div><div class="kb"><div class="kf">Foto<br>2 x 3</div><div class="kd"><div class="nm">'+esc(x.nama)+'</div><table><tr><td>NIPD</td><td>'+pv(x.nipd)+'</td></tr><tr><td>NISN</td><td>'+pv(x.nisn)+'</td></tr><tr><td>TTL</td><td>'+esc((x.tl||"-")+", "+fd(x.tgl))+'</td></tr><tr><td>L/P</td><td>'+pv(x.jk)+'</td></tr><tr><td>Rombel</td><td>'+pv(x.rombel)+'</td></tr></table></div></div><div class="kft">'+esc(SET.area||"")+'</div></div>'}
function doPrint(list,type){if(!list.length){say("Tidak ada siswa untuk dicetak.");return}
var h="";if(type==="kartu"){for(var i=0;i<list.length;i+=10)h+='<div class="kartuWrap">'+list.slice(i,i+10).map(kartuHtml).join("")+'</div>'}else h=list.map(bukuIndukHtml).join("");
PA().innerHTML=h;document.body.classList.add("printing");
var done=function(){document.body.classList.remove("printing");PA().innerHTML="";window.removeEventListener("afterprint",done)};
window.addEventListener("afterprint",done);
setTimeout(function(){try{window.print()}catch(e){done();say("Cetak tidak tersedia di tampilan ini. Buka berkas di browser lalu coba lagi.")}},150)}
function byId(id){return data.filter(function(d){return d.id===id})[0]}
$("vprint").onclick=function(){var x=byId(viewId);if(x){$("dView").close();openPr([x])}};
$("vcard").onclick=function(){var x=byId(viewId);if(x){$("dView").close();doPrint([x],"kartu")}};
$("prAll").onclick=function(){openPr(null)};
$("prGo").onclick=function(){var t=$("prType").value,l=pend||filtered();if(t==="bi"){SET.kop=readKop();SET.kopLogo=pLogo;SET.kopLogoR=pLogoR;saveSet()}$("dPrint").close();doPrint(l,t)};

function kopDef(){return{l1:"",l2:(SET.school||"").toUpperCase(),l3:[SET.area||"",SET.npsn?"NPSN "+SET.npsn:""].filter(Boolean).join(" · "),l4:[SET.tel?"Telp. "+SET.tel:"",SET.eml?"E-mail: "+SET.eml:""].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}}
function kopVal(){var k=SET.kop||{},d=kopDef(),o={};["l1","l2","l3","l4"].forEach(function(f){o[f]=k[f]!=null?k[f]:d[f]});return o}
function kopHtml(v,l,r){var im=function(u){return u?'<img src="'+u+'" alt="">':(l||r?'<div class="kx"></div>':'')};return'<div class="kop">'+im(l)+'<div class="kt">'+(v.l1?'<span class="k1">'+esc(v.l1)+'</span>':'')+'<b>'+esc(v.l2)+'</b>'+(v.l3?'<span>'+esc(v.l3)+'</span>':'')+(v.l4?'<span>'+esc(v.l4)+'</span>':'')+'</div>'+im(r)+'</div>'}
var pend=null,pLogo=null,pLogoR=null;
function readKop(){return{l1:$("kp1").value.trim(),l2:$("kp2").value.trim(),l3:$("kp3").value.trim(),l4:$("kp4").value.trim()}}
function prevKop(){$("kpPrev").innerHTML='<div class="pg-bi" style="width:auto;height:auto;padding:8px 12px;overflow:visible;border:1px dashed #999">'+kopHtml(readKop(),pLogo,pLogoR)+'</div>'}
function fillKop(){var v=kopVal();$("kp1").value=v.l1;$("kp2").value=v.l2;$("kp3").value=v.l3;$("kp4").value=v.l4;pLogo=SET.kopLogo||null;pLogoR=SET.kopLogoR||null;prevKop()}
function togKop(){$("prKop").hidden=$("prType").value!=="bi"}
function openPr(list){pend=list;$("prInfo").textContent=list?("Mencetak buku induk "+list[0].nama+"."):filtered().length+" siswa sesuai pencarian dan filter saat ini akan dicetak.";$("prType").value="bi";$("prType").disabled=!!list;fillKop();togKop();$("dPrint").showModal()}
["kp1","kp2","kp3","kp4"].forEach(function(i){$(i).oninput=prevKop});
$("prType").onchange=togKop;
$("kpLogoDel").onclick=function(){pLogo=null;prevKop()};
$("kpLogoRDel").onclick=function(){pLogoR=null;prevKop()};
$("kpReset").onclick=function(){delete SET.kop;delete SET.kopLogo;delete SET.kopLogoR;saveSet();fillKop();say("Kop dikembalikan ke default.")};
function logoIn(id,set){$(id).onchange=function(){var f=this.files[0];this.value="";if(!f)return;var r=new FileReader();r.onload=function(){var im=new Image();im.onload=function(){var sc=Math.min(1,240/Math.max(im.width,im.height)),c=document.createElement("canvas");c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.width*sc));c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(im.height*sc));c.getContext("2d").drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);set(c.toDataURL("image/png"));prevKop()};im.onerror=function(){say("Gambar logo tidak bisa dibaca.")};im.src=r.result};r.readAsDataURL(f)}}
logoIn("kpLogo",function(u){pLogo=u});logoIn("kpLogoR",function(u){pLogoR=u});
load();render();tab("vDash");
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
